1. In swarm_env.py
School-Zone Velocity Damping: Capped maximum drone velocity to $0.4\text{ m/s}$ (instead of $1.2\text{ m/s}$) when a drone is within $2.5\text{m}$ of the goal to prevent high-speed bottleneck collisions at the funnel.
Dense Separation Reward: Added a continuous penalty of $-2.0 \times (0.6 - d_{\text{neighbor}})$ when any active neighbor is within $0.6\text{m}$ to encourage social distancing.
Progress Reward Scaling: Changed the distance progress reward scaling factor from $0.3$ to $0.5$.
Neighbor Observations (_get_obs): Scaled neighbor distances by COMM_RANGE ($8.0\text{m}$) instead of field width to improve resolution, sorted neighbor slots by proximity (closest first) to guarantee permutation invariance, and included neighbor Dijkstra pathing values.
2. In train.py
Curriculum Restriction: Limited training to Stage 1 only ($d=0.05$ density for 3,000,000 steps) by trimming the curriculum list.
Dynamic Log Prints: Made final checkpoint names and log printouts dynamic based on the curriculum list size (pointing to model_stage1.zip).
